Projection implementation:
```
def project_k(z, target):
    z = np.asarray(z,float)
    lo = -np.max(z) - 1e-12
    hi = 1.0 - np.min(z) + 1e-12
    for _ in range(60):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       s=np.clip(z+mid,0,1).sum()
       if s < target: lo=mid else hi=mid
    h=np.clip(z+(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
    # adjust residual
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if diff !=0: distribute over fractional or all
    return np.clip(h,0,1)
```
Target n/2. If z large, lo/hi good. 60*clip maybe okay but maybe heavy. Can reduce to 40. Projection is called every L-BFGS eval; need speed. We can find λ by sorted values O(N log N) once, but z changes. Bisection 40*N=16k, for N=400 okay. For N=800, 32k. If 5k evals, 160M operations maybe okay in C? Numpy clip on 800 arrays 40 times = 32k elements per eval, 5k=160M, maybe seconds. Fine.

Function:
```
_ALPHA=100.0; _BETA=0.2; _N=400
def soft_func(x):
    h = soft_project(x, _BETA, _N//2)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
    val, _ = logsum(c, _ALPHA)
    return float(val)
def soft_jac(x):
    h = soft_project(x, _BETA, _N//2)
    c = np.correlate(...)
    val,w = logsum(c, _ALPHA)
    G = weight_grad(h,w)
    p = h*(1-h)
    S = p.sum()
    if S<1e-14: return np.zeros(_N)
    mean = (G*p).sum()/S
    return (p/_BETA)*(G-mean)
```
Note objective smooth count units (counts) not dx. The final C = val * dx (where val ~ max count). For alpha close ∞, val -> max count. Good.

def sigmoid(y):
    out=np.empty_like(y)
    pos=y>=0
    out[pos]=1/(1+np.exp(-y[pos]))
    ey=np.exp(y[~pos]); out[~pos]=ey/(1+ey)
    return out
```

Steepest binary sweep algorithm:
```
def binary_steepest(h0,n,deadline,max_sweeps=50):
  h=to_binary(h0); corr=...
  best...
  while time<deadline:
    zeros=np.where(h==0)[0]; ones=np.where(h==1)[0]
    A=build_A(zeros,h); B=build_B(ones,h)
    best_move=None; best_val=cur_c
    for start in range(0,len(zeros),block):
       Ab=A[start:start+block]
       M = c[None,None,:] + Ab[:,:,None] + B[None,:,:]  # memory
       M[:, :, t0] -= 2.0
       flat = M.max(axis=2).ravel()
       # retrieve worst? We want min (lowest new max). Actually min of new max. base_min. Get idx = argmin
       # to account corrections, get top K indices (smallest base) e.g. 20
       part = np.argpartition(flat, K)[:K]; candidates = part[np.argsort(flat[part])]
       for flatidx in candidates:
          ai=..., bi=...
          i=zeros[start+ai]; j=ones[bi]
          delta = ... exact (O(L))
          nm = (corr+delta).max()*dx
          if nm < best_val: best...
    if none: break
    apply best, corr += delta, cur_c=...
    update best
  return
```

`soft_project`:
```
def soft_project(z, beta, target):
    mu = _MU_LAST.copy()
    # initial mu maybe median(z)
    if not np.isfinite(mu): mu=np.median(z)
    # bracket around current if needed
    lo = np.min(z) - 10*beta
    hi = np.max(z) + 10*beta
    # ensure S at lo>=target and hi<=target; if not expand
    for _ in range(2):
       if soft_sum(z,lo,beta) < target: lo -= 10*beta
       if soft_sum(z,hi,beta) > target: hi += 10*beta
    # Use current mu if inside, else median
    mu = min(max(mu, lo), hi)
    # Newton maybe:
    for it in range(6):
       y=(z-mu)/beta; h=expit(np.clip(y,-50,50)); s=h.sum()
       diff=s-target
       if abs(diff) < 1e-9*max(1,target): break
       p=h*(1-h); sp = -p.sum()/beta
       if sp==0 or not finite: break
       step = diff/sp
       # damp
       if step > 0.5*(hi-mu): step=0.5*(hi-mu)
       if step < -0.5*(mu-lo): step=-0.5*(mu-lo)
       mu -= step
       if mu<lo: mu=lo; break
       if mu>hi: mu=hi; break
    # final bisection maybe 20 to ensure
    lo,hi = mu - 2*beta, mu + 2*beta? Need bracket.
    # Easier final: if sum at mu close, else bracket original and bisection 25.
```
Newton may fail; final fallback bisection over global bracket (30). Could just bisection always. Simpler less bug. Use 40 bisection with expit, okay.

Implement weight_grad loops efficiently:
```
def weighted_grad(h, w):
    n=len(h)
    G=np.zeros(n)
    u=1.0-h
    L=2*n-1
    # w already length L
    for s in range(-n+1, n):
       ws = w[n-1+s]
       if ws == 0.0: continue
       if s >= 0:
           length = n - s
           G[:length] += ws * u[s:]
           G[s:] -= ws * h[:length]
       else:
           length = n - 1 + s
           # first term none? Actually A only s>=0. But G formula first term has s>=0 only. For s<0, B only:
           G[:length] -= ws * h[-s:]
```

F_s = ∑_{x: 0≤x<N, 0≤x+s<N} h_x(1-h_{x+s}). For s negative, overlap x from -s to N-1, i.e., length N+? Actually if s=-a, x from a to N-1 length N-a. The derivative as first: for x such that x+s valid -> x≥-s. term (1-h_{x+s}). As second: for x such that x-s valid -> x≤s? Wait p as second index p=x+s; if p=x? So G[p]=∑_{s: p+s valid} w_s(1-h[p+s]) - ∑_{s: p-s valid} w_s h[p-s]. These are the same. For s negative in first term: p+s valid if p≥-s and p+s<N (always if p<N and s neg, but p+s>=0). Our earlier A loop only s>=0 for first was wrong. Need include negative. Let's fix.

For each s, first segment:
- if s >=0: p=0..N-1-s, src=p+s=s..N-1. length N-s.
- if s <0: p=-s..N-1, src=0..N-1+s. length N+s? N+s (since s=-a, N-a). 
Second segment:
- if s >=0: p=s..N-1, src=0..N-1-s. length N-s.
- if s <0: p=0..N-1+s, src=-s..N-1. length N+s.
Then G[p] += first - second.

This is O(N^2) with 2 nonzero checks. Can implement loops over s with slices:
```
for s in range(-n+1,n):
  ws=w[n-1+s]
  if ws==0: continue
  if s>=0:
     l = n-s
     G[:l] += ws*(1.0-h[s:])
     G[s:] -= ws*h[:l]
  else:
     a=-s
     l=n-a
     G[a:] += ws*(1.0-h[:l])
     G[:l] -= ws*h[a:]
```

Soft L-BFGS details:
```
def optimize_soft(h, n, alpha, beta, z=None, maxiter=500, maxfun=5000, ftol=1e-16, deadline=None):
   global _BETA,_ALPHA,_N
   _TYPES
   if z is None:
       hc = np.clip(h,1e-6,1-1e-6)
       z = beta*np.log(hc/(1-hc))
       # center z to reduce magnitude: z -= np.mean(z)
   else:
       z = z.copy()
   # add small noise maybe
   fun = soft_func; jac=soft_jac
   opts={'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxfun,'ftol':ftol,'gtol':1e-12,'maxls':10}
   res=minimize(fun,z,method='L-BFGS-B',jac=jac,options=opts)
   h2=soft_project(res.x,beta,n/2)
   # fix sum and bounds
   h2=fix_sum(h2,n)
   c=compute count max
   return h2, count, res.x
```

Now binary steepest with D vector. Build A/B efficiently.
```
def build_AB(zeros, ones, h):
  n=len(h); L=2*n-1
  zc=len(zeros); oc=len(ones)
  A=np.zeros((zc,L))
  B=np.zeros((oc,L))
  h_rev = h[::-1]
  one_minus = 1.0 - h
  for k,p in enumerate(zeros):
      st = n-1-p
      A[k, st:st+n] += one_minus # length n
      A[k, p:p+n] += -h_rev
  for k,p in enumerate(ones):
      st = n-1-p
      B[k, st:st+n] += -one_minus
      B[k, p:p+n] += h_rev
  return A,B
```
Need slices endpoints: st+n ≤ L? st max n-1 (p=0) => 2n-1 = L, ok. p+n ≤ L? p=n-1 =>2n-1, ok. Wait segment length N, start p for p=0 ->0..N? okay. But for first segment, start n-1-p: if p=0 start n-1 end 2n-1 exclusive -> indices n-1..2n-2 length n. If p=n-1 start0 end n length n. Good. A initialized zeros; add two segments may overlap; correct. For zero p, at s=0 first segment offset p? first start n-1-p, offset p value 1-h[p]=1; second start p, offset n-1-p value h_rev[n-1-p]=h[p]=0; sum 1. For one B at s=0: first value -(1-h[p])=0, second value h_rev[n-1-p]=h[p]=1 sum1. Good.

def exact_new_c(c, Arow, Brow, i, j, n):
    w = c + Arow + Brow
    t0=n-1
    w[t0] -= 2.0
    w[n-1 + (j-i)] += 1.0
    w[n-1 + (i-j)] += 1.0
    return float(w.max())
```

If sum != target, distribute difference to entries with room. Since h from interpolation near feasible, small. Use previous `project_feasible` but more exact:
```
def fix_feasible(h,n):
 target=n/2
 h=np.clip(h,0,1)
 for _ in range(100):
   diff=target-h.sum()
   if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
   if diff>0:
      mask=h<1
      if not mask.any(): break
      room=1-h; h[mask]+=diff*room[mask]/room[mask].sum()
   else:
      mask=h>0; h[mask]+=diff*h[mask]/h[mask].sum()
 return np.clip(h,0,1)
```
Then adjust residual on largest fractional? Because multiplicative may hit bounds. Could use L2 projection exactly with bisection as `project_k` with z=h. Let's implement `project_k` and use for fix. It returns h with sum target within bisection. Then final exact micro adjust.

Thus gradient can be computed with two C-level correlations/convolutions of lengths N and L, much faster! Let's verify with small example? We can trust.

So:
```
w = weights length L centered index s+N-1.
u=1-h
A = np.correlate(u, w, 'full')[N-1:2*N-1]  # length N
B = np.convolve(h, w, 'full')[N-1:2*N-1]
G = A - B
```

Let's implement `make_grad(h,w,n)` with C functions:
```
def make_grad(h,w,n):
    u = 1.0 - h
    A = np.correlate(u, w, mode='full')[n-1:2*n-1]
    B = np.convolve(h, w, mode='full')[n-1:2*n-1]
    return A - B
```

```
def soft_lbfgs(...):
  global _BETA, _ALPHA, _N, _TARGET
  _N=n; _TARGET=n/2; _BETA=beta; _ALPHA=alpha
  if z is None:
     hc=np.clip(h,1e-4,1-1e-4)
     z=beta*np.log(hc/(1-hc))
     z -= z.mean()
     if noise>0: z += noise*np.random.RandomState(seed).randn(n)
  else:
     z=np.array(z,float).copy()
     z -= z.mean()
  if not all finite: fallback
  # L-BFGS-B bounds maybe [-8/beta? Actually z scale; set bounds=[(-8,8)]*n? For beta .1, z range ±8 corresponds h 0.0003..0.9997. Good. Use -10,10.
  res=minimize(soft_func,z,jac=soft_grad,bounds=[(-10,10)]*n, method='L-BFGS-B', options={...})
  h2=soft_project(res.x,beta,n/2)
  h2=np.clip(h2,0,1)
  h2=fix_sum(h2,n)
  return h2
```

```
def soft_func(x):
    h = soft_project(np.asarray(x), _BETA, _TARGET)
    c = corr_D(h)
    val = logsum_value(c, _ALPHA)
    return val
def soft_grad(x):
    h=soft_project(np.asarray(x), _BETA, _TARGET)
    c=corr_D(h)
    val,w = logsum_weights(c,_ALPHA)
    G=make_grad(h,w,_N)
    p=h*(1-h)
    S=p.sum()
    if S<1e-12:
       return np.zeros(_N)
    mean = (G*p).sum()/S
    return (p/_BETA)*(G-mean)
```

Let's implement `soft_project` with bisection 40:
```
def soft_project(z,beta,target):
    z=np.asarray(z,float)
    lo = float(np.min(z)) - 12.0*beta
    hi = float(np.max(z)) + 12.0*beta
    # ensure hi > lo
    for _ in range(40):
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
       y=(z-mid)/beta
       # expit stable
       s=float(np.sum(np.expit(np.clip(y,-50,50))))
       if s > target:
           lo=mid
       else:
           hi=mid
    mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
    return np.expit(np.clip((z-mid)/beta,-50,50))
```
Check bracketing: At lo = min(z)-12β, y around [ -12β? for min, +range/beta]. If range maybe large 40, y large for max -> sum near n. So s>target likely. At hi max+12β, y max 12, min -range/β; sum near 0. Good. If range huge (z bounds ±20, beta .05 => range 400, lo min-0.6 -> max y ~8000, sum n; hi max+0.6 -> min y -8000, sum0). Good.
Bisection condition: We want root where s(target). If s>target, need increase mid, so lo=mid. If s<target, hi=mid. At end. Good.
40 iterations enough. Could use 35.

Potential bug: If beta very small and z range huge, expit clip to ±50, sum at lo maybe not n if some y >50 all? yes n; hi 0. Good.

`fix_sum`:
```
def fix_sum(h,n):
   target=n/2
   h=np.clip(h,0,1)
   diff=target-h.sum()
   if abs(diff)>0:
      # distribute to entries with room
      if diff>0:
         mask=h<1
         if mask.any():
            room=1-h
            rsum=room.sum()
            if rsum>0:
               h[mask]+=diff*room[mask]/rsum
      else:
         mask=h>0
         if mask.any():
            rsum=h.sum()
            if rsum>0:
               h[mask]+=diff*h[mask]/rsum
   h=np.clip(h,0,1)
   # exact single adjustment
   diff=target-h.sum()
   if diff != 0:
      if diff>0:
         i=int(np.argmax(1-h))
         h[i]=min(1.0,h[i]+diff)
      else:
         i=int(np.argmin(h))
         h[i]=max(0.0,h[i]+diff) # diff negative? want subtract? Actually diff = target - sum, negative if sum too high. Need add diff (negative) to i.
   return h
```
For diff negative, `h[i] += diff` (decrease). Use max. If diff >1? not.

But if we add diff to argmax room, maybe if diff > room? not. Good.

Now, alternative: implement custom Adam with our fast gradient; can run for fixed time, no large call overrun. It may complement L-BFGS. Maybe easier to control and can do many small steps. Let's consider adding an Adam polish after L-BFGS.

Custom soft Adam:
```
def soft_adam(h,z,beta,alpha,deadline,lr=0.5):
   global set
   m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); mhat/vhat biases
   for t in range(1,int(1e6)):
      if time>deadline: break
      grad=soft_grad(z,beta,alpha)  (manual loop not through globals? We can write function taking args)
      m = bm*m+(1-bm)*grad; v=bv*v+(1-bv)*grad*grad
      mhat=m/(1-bm**t); vhat=v/(1-bv**t)
      step=lr*mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
      z = np.clip(z - step, -20,20)
      # maybe occasional evaluate and project
      if t%50==0:

         count=max(corr_D(h)); update best
   return best
```

Detailed code plan:
```
import numpy as np, time
from scipy.optimize import minimize
from scipy.special import expit

_ALPHA=1.0; _BETA=1.0; _N=1; _TARGET=0.5
_CACHE=...

def corr_D(h): return np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')[::-1]

def count_max(h): return float(np.max(corr_D(h)))

def c5_from_count(count,n): return count*2.0/n

def logsum_count(c, alpha):
   m = float(np.max(c))
   if alpha <= 0: return m, np.zeros_like(c)
   sh = alpha*(c-m)
   # sh<=0
   e = np.exp(sh)
   s = float(e.sum())
   if s <=0: # fallback
       w=np.zeros_like(c); w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0; return m,w
   w=e/s
   return m + np.log(s)/alpha, w
```
Note if alpha very large and m maybe 400, sh for max 0, others underflow; s≥1.

`make_grad(h,w,n)`:
```
u = 1.0-h
A = np.correlate(u, w, mode='full')[n-1:2*n-1]
B = np.convolve(h, w, mode='full')[n-1:2*n-1]
return A - B
```
Need ensure outputs length n. For n=1? not.

`soft_project(z,beta,target)`:
```
z=np.asarray(z,float)
lo = float(np.min(z)) - 12*beta
hi = float(np.max(z)) + 12*beta
if hi <= lo: hi=lo+1
for _ in range(38):
   mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
   y=(z-mid)/beta
   y=np.clip(y,-50,50)
   s=float(np.sum(expit(y)))
   if s > target:
      lo=mid
   else:
      hi=mid
mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
y=np.clip((z-mid)/beta,-50,50)
return expit(y)
```
38 iterations. If beta maybe 0? ensure beta>1e-6.

`soft_objective(x)`:
```
h=soft_project(x,_BETA,_TARGET)
c=corr_D(h)
val,w=logsum_count(c,_ALPHA)
return val
def soft_jacobian(x):
   h=soft_project(x,_BETA,_TARGET)
   c=corr_D(h)
   _,w=logsum_count(c,_ALPHA)
   G=make_grad(h,w,_N)
   p=h*(1-h)
   S=float(p.sum())
   if S < 1e-14:
      return np.zeros(_N)
   mean = float((G*p).sum()/S)
   return (p/_BETA)*(G-mean)
```
Potential global `_N` not updated for n? Set.

`soft_lbfgs(h,n,beta,alpha,maxfun,seed=None,noise=0.0)`:
```
global _ALPHA,_BETA,_N,_TARGET
_N=n; _TARGET=n/2; _BETA=max(beta,1e-3); _ALPHA=alpha
hc=np.clip(h,1e-5,1-1e-5)
if noise is None:
   z=beta*np.log(hc/(1-hc))
else:
   z=...
rng=np.random.RandomState(seed if seed is not None else 1)
if noise:
   z += noise*beta*rng.randn(n)
z -= float(np.mean(z))
# If z has inf/nan: set zeros
if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)): z=np.zeros(n)
bounds=[(-20.0,20.0)]*n
opts={'maxiter':200, 'maxfun':maxfun, 'ftol':1e-18, 'gtol':1e-10, 'maxcor':10, 'maxls':5}
try:
   res=minimize(soft_objective, z, jac=soft_jacobian, bounds=bounds, method='L-BFGS-B', options=opts)
   h2=soft_project(res.x, beta, _TARGET)
except Exception:
   h2=h
h2=fix_feasible(h2,n)
return h2
```

`fix_feasible(h,n)` as above. Use projection L2? We can implement project_k for better. Since used in final and interpolation, not in soft. Let's write L2 projection:
```
def project_k(z,target):
   z=np.asarray(z,float)
   lo=float(np.min(z))-1e-9
   hi=1.0-float(np.min(z))+1e-9? Wait for clip(z+t). t= -λ. Sum < target at low t. Use lo= -max(z)-1e-6, hi=1-min(z)+1e-6.
   for 50:
      mid=(lo+hi)/2
      s=float(np.sum(np.clip(z+mid,0,1)))
      if s < target: lo=mid
      else: hi=mid
   h=np.clip(z+(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
   return h
def fix_feasible(h,n):
   h=project_k(h,n/2)
   # exact adjust
   diff=n/2 - h.sum()
   if diff>0:
      idx=int(np.argmax(h<1?)) # argmax(1-h)
      h[idx]=min(1,h[idx]+diff)
   elif diff<0:
      idx=int(np.argmin(h))
      h[idx]=max(0,h[idx]+diff)
   return h
```
Check project_k bracket: t low = -max(z)-1, all z+t <=0? If max(z)=0.5, t=-1.5, max z+t=-1 ->0. min negative. sum0 < target. t high=1-min(z)+1, min z+t>=1, sum n > target. So if s<target, increase t (lo=mid); else hi=mid. Good. At end. Use 50 iterations.

But `project_k` of an already feasible h should return h? If h sum target, t=0 feasible; bisection may converge to 0 if unique? If h has bounds and sum target, yes. But if multiple due to flat? still any feasible? Actually projection unique but if feasible, itself. Bisection for t sum target may find t=0 if h within; if h has entries exactly 0/1 and sum target, yes.

`binary_steepest(h0,n,deadline,max_sweeps=100)`:
```
def binary_steepest(...):
  h=to_binary(h0,n).astype(float)
  c=corr_D(h)
  cur=float(c.max()); best_h=h.copy(); best_count=cur
  if len(h)<2: return
  t0=n-1; L=2*n-1
  ones=np.where(h==1)[0].tolist? use arrays
  no_improve=0
  while time.time()<deadline and no_improve<3:
     zeros=np.where(h==0)[0]
     ones=np.where(h==1)[0]
     if zeros.size<1 or ones.size<1: break
     A,B=build_AB(zeros,ones,h)
     best_count_local=cur
     best_i=-1; best_j=-1; best_delta=None
     # choose block size
     block=10
     for st in range(0,len(zeros),block):
        en=min(st+block,len(zeros))
        Ab=A[st:en]
        # if time? check occasionally
        M = c[None,None,:] + Ab[:,:,None] + B[None,:,:]
        M[:,:,t0] -= 2.0
        scores = M.max(axis=2)
        flat = scores.ravel()
        K=30
        if len(flat)>K:
           part = np.argpartition(flat, K-1)[:K]
           part = part[np.argsort(flat[part])]
        else:
           part = np.arange(len(flat))
        for ci in part:
           ai=int(ci)//ones.size; bi=int(ci)%ones.size
           i=int(zeros[st+ai]); j=int(ones[bi])
           base = c + A[st+ai] + B[bi]
           base[t0] -= 2.0
           base[t0 + j - i] += 1.0
           base[t0 + i - j] += 1.0
           nm=float(base.max())
           if nm < best_count_local - 1e-12:
              best_count_local=nm; best_i=i; best_j=j; best_delta=base - c
      if best_i<0:
         no_improve+=1; continue? If no improvement, break to avoid loop.
      h[best_i]=1; h[best_j]=0
      c = corr_D(h)
      cur=float(c.max())
      no_improve=0
      if cur < best_count:
         best_count=cur; best_h=h.copy()
  return best_h,best_count
```
If no improvement, break not continue. `continue` would infinite. So break.

`build_AB(zeros, ones, h)`:
Need zeros/ones arrays; h float; `h_rev = h[::-1]`; `omu = 1.0 - h`.
```
def build_AB(zeros, ones, h):
  n=len(h); L=2*n-1
  A=np.zeros((len(zeros), L), dtype=float)
  B=np.zeros((len(ones), L), dtype=float)
  hr = h[::-1]
  omu = 1.0-h
  for k,p in enumerate(zeros):
     p=int(p); st=n-1-p
     A[k, st:st+n] += omu
     A[k, p:p+n] += -hr
  for k,p in enumerate(ones):
     p=int(p); st=n-1-p
     B[k, st:st+n] += -omu
     B[k, p:p+n] += hr
  return A,B
```
Slices with `st:st+n` length n; `p:p+n` length n. Good. This uses Python loops zeros+ones= n. For n=500, 1000 slice adds each sweep. Acceptable. Could optimize by prealloc and fancy? fine.

Potential bug: first/second segments both length n, but for p near edges, segment overlaps exactly? For p=0 first n-1..2n-2, second 0..n-1; all. Correct. For p=n-1 first 0..n-1, second n-1..2n-2. Good.

`make_initial` returns binary balanced? Let's implement:
```
def make_initial(n, seed):
    x=np.arange(n)
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    # blend low-discrepancy and random
    vals=(x*0.61803398875 + rng.rand(n)*0.1) % 1.0
    order=np.argsort(vals)
    h=np.zeros(n)
    h[order[n//2:]]=1.0  # top half
    return h
```
Sum exactly n/2. Good. For deterministic if no seed? seed given.

Could also block initial: h[h?]. Fine.

```
def snap_feasible(h,n):
   h=np.where(h<1e-8,0.0,h)
   h=np.where(h>1-1e-8,1.0,h)
   h=fix_feasible(h,n)
   return h
```

```

```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize
from scipy.special import expit

_ALPHA=1.0
_BETA=1.0
_N=1
_TARGET=0.5

def corr_d(h):...
...
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    deadline=t0 + max(60.0, float(budget_s)-12.0)
    try:
       n=len(initial_h_values)
       h0=np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
    except Exception:
       n=400
       h0=make_initial(n, seed)
    n=int(n)
    h0=fix_feasible(h0,n)
    c=corr_d(h0); best_cnt=float(c.max()); best_c=best_cnt*2.0/n; best_h=h0.copy()
    # transforms
    for cand in (1.0-h0, h0[::-1], 1.0-h0[::-1]):
       cand=fix_feasible(cand,n)
       cnt=float(np.max(corr_d(cand)))
       if cnt < best_cnt:
          best_cnt=cnt; best_c=cnt*2.0/n; best_h=cand.copy()
    total=deadline-t0
    # binary phase
    p_bin=t0+min(150.0, max(40.0, 0.18*total))
    if p_bin < deadline and time.time() < p_bin-5:
       hb,bcnt=binary_steepest(best_h,n,p_bin-5,max_sweeps=200)
       if bcnt<best_cnt: update
       if time.time() < p_bin-20:
          h_alt=make_initial(n, seed+101)
          hb2,bcnt2=binary_steepest(h_alt,n,p_bin-20,max_sweeps=80)
          if bcnt2<best_cnt: update
    # main continuous plan
    cont_dead=deadline - max(90.0, 0.18*total) if total > 400 else deadline - 30.0
    if time.time() < cont_dead:
       hwork=best_h.copy()
       plan = [ (1.0,10.0,0.0), (0.5,100.0,0.0), (0.25,1000.0,0.0), (0.12,1e4,0.0), (0.06,1e5,0.0), (0.03,1e6,0.0), (0.12,1e4,0.05), (0.06,1e5,0.05), (0.03,1e6,0.05) ]
       for idx in range(len(plan)):
          if time.time() > cont_dead - 25: break
          beta, alpha, noise = plan[idx]
          mf = 500 if n <= 450 else 300
          if cont_dead - time.time() < 120: mf = 200
          hnew=soft_lbfgs(hwork,n,beta,alpha,mf,seed=seed+idx,noise=noise)
          hnew=fix_feasible(hnew,n)
          cnt=float(np.max(corr_d(hnew)))
          if cnt < best_cnt:
             best_cnt=cnt; best_c=cnt*2.0/n; best_h=hnew.copy(); hwork=best_h.copy()
          elif cnt < best_cnt + 0.25:
             hwork=hnew.copy()
       # binary-fresh continuous
       if time.time() < cont_dead - 25:
          hb=to_binary(best_h,n)
          hnew=soft_lbfgs(hb,n,0.06,1e5,300,seed=seed+20,noise=0.03)
          ... update
    # higher res
    # determine candidates
    tried = set([n]) # no set? can use list
    if time.time() < deadline - 80:
       if n <= 550:
          n2 = n + 100
          if n2 <= 700 and n2 != n:
             h2=interpolate_h(best_h,n,n2)
             # quick optimize
             for beta,alpha in ((0.15,1e4),(0.06,1e5)):
                if time.time() > deadline - 40: break
                hnew=soft_lbfgs(h2,n2,beta,alpha,250,seed=seed+n2,noise=0.0)
                cnt=...; cval=cnt*2.0/n2
                if cval < best_c:
                   best_c=cval; best_h=hnew.copy(); best_cnt=cnt; n=n2; h2=best_h.copy()
                   break? continue with new n
             # maybe binary at n2? if time
       # second higher n if time
       if time.time() < deadline - 70 and n < 700:
          n3 = n + 100
          if n3 <= 800:
             h3=interpolate_h(best_h,n,n3)
             ... one call
    # final polish
    for beta,alpha,mf,noise in ((0.03,1e6,500,0.0),(0.02,1e6,400,0.0),(0.05,1e5,300,0.02)):
       if time.time() > deadline - 20: break
       hnew=soft_lbfgs(best_h,n,beta,alpha,mf,seed=seed+1000,noise=noise)
       hnew=fix_feasible(hnew,n)
       cnt=...; if cnt*2/n < best_c: update
    # snap check
    hs=snap_feasible(best_h,n)
    cnt=...; if cnt*2/n < best_c + 1e-12: # choose snap if better or equal?
       best_h=hs; best_cnt=cnt; best_c=...
    best_h=fix_feasible(best_h,n)
    best_c=float(np.max(corr_d(best_h))*2.0/n)
    return best_h,best_c,n
```

def to_feasible_discrete(h,n):
  target=n/2
  hb=np.zeros(n)
  k=int(target)
  rem=target-k
  order=np.argsort(h)[::-1]
  if rem>0:
     hb[order[k]]=0.5? Then sum k+0.5.
     hb[order[:k]]=1
  else:
     hb[order[:k]]=1
  return hb
```

Let's draft fully:

```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize
from scipy.special import expit

_ALPHA=1.0; _BETA=1.0; _N=1; _TARGET=0.5

def corr_d(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')

def logsum_count(c, alpha):
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if alpha <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return m, w
    sh = alpha * (c - m)
    e = np.exp(np.minimum(sh, 0.0))
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return m, w
    return m + float(np.log(s)) / alpha, e / s
```
`np.minimum(sh,0)` handles tiny positive due to rounding.

`make_grad` as above.

`project_k`:
```
def project_k(z, target):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=float)
    lo = -float(np.max(z)) - 1e-9
    hi = 1.0 - float(np.min(z)) + 1e-9
    if hi <= lo: hi = lo + 1.0
    for _ in range(45):
        mid = 0.5*(lo+hi)
        s = float(np.sum(np.clip(z + mid, 0.0, 1.0)))
        if s < target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    return np.clip(z + 0.5*(lo+hi), 0.0, 1.0)
```
If z has NaN, max NaN; not.

`fix_feasible`:
```
def fix_feasible(h, n):
    target = 0.5 * n
    h = project_k(h, target)
    diff = target - float(h.sum())
    if diff > 0.0:
        if diff > 0.0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
            add = min(diff, 1.0 - h[idx])
            h[idx] += add
    elif diff < 0.0:
        idx = int(np.argmin(h))
        sub = min(-diff, h[idx])
        h[idx] -= sub
    return np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
```
After adjusting, due to clipping sum might not exact if diff exceeded room (shouldn't). Could loop? Maybe after adjustment diff tiny. To be safe, after one adjust, if residual, distribute? Use while small? Add a second pass:
```
    for _ in range(3):
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
       if diff>0: idx=argmax(1-h); h[idx]=min(1,h[idx]+diff)
       else: idx=argmin(h); h[idx]=max(0,h[idx]+diff)
```
Use loop.

`snap_feasible`:
```
def snap_feasible(h,n):
   h=np.where(h<1e-8,0.0,h)
   h=np.where(h>1-1e-8,1.0,h)
   return fix_feasible(h,n)
```

`make_initial`:
```
def make_initial(n, seed):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    x = np.arange(n)
    vals = (x * 0.6180339887498949 + rng.rand(n) * 0.1) % 1.0
    order = np.argsort(vals)
    h = np.zeros(n)
    h[order[n//2:]] = 1.0
    return h
```
If n not even, sum floor. For continuous target maybe half. Later fix_feasible? make_initial returns binary sum floor if odd. For even fine. Could set one 0.5 if odd:

rem = n/2 - int(n//2)

```

`to_binary`:
```
def to_binary(h,n):
    k = int(np.floor(0.5*n))
    h_b = np.zeros(n)
    order = np.argsort(h)[::-1]
    h_b[order[:k]] = 1.0
    if 0.5*n - k > 0:
        h_b[order[k]] = 0.5*n - k  # but can't >1
    return h_b
```
For even no frac. For odd, set 0.5; binary steepest swaps flips 0.5? It assumes zeros/ones lists. It may not handle 0.5. n even. Could if odd use fix. Not worry.

def interpolate_h(h,n,n2):
    x1 = (np.arange(n)+0.5)*(2.0/n)
    x2 = (np.arange(n2)+0.5)*(2.0/n2)
    h2 = np.interp(x2, x1, h)
    return fix_feasible(h2, n2)
```

final_plan = ((0.03, 1.0e6, 400, 0.0), (0.02, 1.0e7, 250, 0.0), (0.06, 1.0e5, 250, 0.02))

Let's think about exact sum in soft h: `soft_project` bisection 40 iterations. If target half-integer, sum error maybe 1e-10. fix. Good.

Now, perhaps `project_k` exact adjust loop: `diff = target - float(h.sum())`; if diff>0, `idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))`; add=diff (but if 1-h[idx] inf?). h finite. h[idx] may be 1? If all 1 and diff>0 impossible. add min. If h[idx] + add >1, clip? We do `h[idx] = min(1.0, h[idx] + add)`. The loop repeats if residual. Good. For diff<0, idx argmin h; `h[idx] = max(0.0, h[idx] + diff)` (diff negative). Good.

Now, ensure `budget_s` can be None? default. Use float(budget_s) maybe if None error. Not.

Now, if `time.time()` near deadline, final fix and compute still take <1s. Good.

Let's produce final. But before final, one more sanity check of gradient with weight vector from logsum of D. Suppose active shift s only weight 1. Gradient G = grad_D_s. For a continuous move in soft z, formula p/β(G - weighted_mean). Weighted mean of grad over p? Here p weights by h(1-h). Is that correct for sum constraint? Yes. But wait for a single shift D_s, the constraint sum h = target. The feasible tangent directions have sum zero in h. The parameterization h=p? The derivative dh_i/dz_j = p_i/β(δ_ij - p_j/S)? Let's re-derive one more time: h_i=σ((z_i-μ)/β), μ solves ∑h=target. ∂μ/∂z_j = ∂h_j/∂z_j? From constraint: 0=∑_i ∂h_i/∂μ δμ + ∂h_j/∂z_j. ∂h_i/∂μ = -p_i/β, ∂h_j/∂z_j = p_j/β. Thus δμ = p_j/S. Then ∂h_i/∂z_j = p_i/β δ_ij - p_i/β * p_j/S = p_i/β(δ_ij - p_j/S). Then gradient wrt z_j = ∑ G_i ∂h_i/∂z_j = (p_j/β)(G_j - (∑ G_i p_i / S? Wait ∑ G_i p_i (δ_ij - p_j/S) = G_j p_j - p_j ∑G_i p_i. So = p_j (G_j - ∑G_i p_i). Missing 1/S? Since p_i/β(δ_ij - p_j/S): ∑ G_i p_i/β (δ_ij - p_j/S) = (p_j/β)G_j - (p_j/β)(p_j? no, p_j not p_i) Wait ∑ G_i p_i δ_ij = G_j p_j. Second: ∑ G_i p_i * p_j / S = p_j/S ∑G_i p_i. So gradient = (p_j/β)(G_j - mean_p(G)). Yes.
But note ∂h_i/∂z_j formula has p_i (not p_j). Summing gives p_j? Let's recalc: ∂h_i/∂z_j = p_i/β δ_ij - p_i/β * p_j/S. Sum G_i: first G_j p_j/β. second p_j/S ∑ G_i p_i /β. = p_j/β (G_j - mean). Correct. Good.
If p_j=0 at boundary, derivative zero. Good.

Now, objective F(z) invariant under z+c; ∑ grad_j = 1/β(∑p_j G_j - S mean)=0. Good.

Now, if `p` all equal, mean ordinary, gradient p/β(G-avg). Projection L-BFGS active all would be G-avg; same. Good.

Now, if beta small, z variable scale is beta*logit. Starting `z=beta*logit(h)`. Gradient large in interior. L-BFGS may prefer large beta. We schedule down. Good.

Could we remove 1/beta from gradient and treat z as logit not scaled? If z=logit h, beta=1 in parameter h=sigmoid((z-μ)/β)? If β fixed, starting z maybe logit h *β? We use z=β logit. If we instead use z=logit h and parameter h=sigmoid((z-μ)), beta=1 always, gradient p(G-mean). This avoids 1/β. Beta could be introduced as width in h= sigmoid((z-μ)/β) with z not scaled: z=logit h, gradient p/β. Starting z not scaled; for small beta, gradient large. We did same. Could scale gradient by beta to keep step? But then z dynamics differ. L-BFGS adapts.

Maybe using beta<1 increases gradient magnitude and makes line search difficult. Alternative use beta>1 for smooth, then beta<1 only via scaling? If beta small, p/β large. But p small near binary; in interior p~0.25, beta=0.03 gradient ~8*G. L-BFGS can handle with line search. If too large, might fail. Could set betas not too small: final 0.05. Plan uses 0.03. okay.

Now, if line search fails, minimize may return initial. We check improvement. Good.

Now, final code. Ensure the code block only. Let's proceed.
</think>

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize
from scipy.special import expit

_ALPHA = 1.0
_BETA = 1.0
_N = 1
_TARGET = 0.5


def corr_d(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')


def logsum_count(c, alpha):
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if alpha <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return m, w
    sh = np.minimum(alpha * (c - m), 0.0)
    e = np.exp(sh)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return m, w
    return m + float(np.log(s)) / alpha, e / s


def make_grad(h, w, n):
    u = 1.0 - h
    a = np.correlate(u, w, mode='full')[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    b = np.convolve(h, w, mode='full')[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    return a - b


def project_k(z, target):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=float)
    lo = -float(np.max(z)) - 1e-9
    hi = 1.0 - float(np.min(z)) + 1e-9
    if hi <= lo:
        hi = lo + 1.0
    for _ in range(45):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.sum(np.clip(z + mid, 0.0, 1.0)))
        if s < target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    return np.clip(z + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)


def fix_feasible(h, n):
    target = 0.5 * n
    h = project_k(h, target)
    for _ in range(3):
        diff = target - float(h.sum())
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
            h[idx] = min(1.0, h[idx] + diff)
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmin(h))
            h[idx] = max(0.0, h[idx] + diff)
    return np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)


def snap_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.where(h < 1e-8, 0.0, h)
    h = np.where(h > 1.0 - 1e-8, 1.0, h)
    return fix_feasible(h, n)


def make_initial(n, seed):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    x = np.arange(n)
    vals = (x * 0.6180339887498949 + rng.rand(n) * 0.1) % 1.0
    order = np.argsort(vals)
    h = np.zeros(n)
    h[order[n // 2:]] = 1.0
    return h


def to_binary(h, n):
    k = int(np.floor(0.5 * n))
    hb = np.zeros(n)
    order = np.argsort(h)[::-1]
    hb[order[:k]] = 1.0
    rem = 0.5 * n - k
    if rem > 1e-12:
        hb[order[k]] = rem
    return hb


def build_ab(zeros, ones, h):
    n = len(h)
    l = 2 * n - 1
    a = np.zeros((len(zeros), l), dtype=float)
    b = np.zeros((len(ones), l), dtype=float)
    hr = h[::-1]
    omu = 1.0 - h
    for r, p in enumerate(zeros):
        p = int(p)
        st = n - 1 - p
        a[r, st:st + n] += omu
        a[r, p:p + n] += -hr
    for r, p in enumerate(ones):
        p = int(p)
        st = n - 1 - p
        b[r, st:st + n] += -omu
        b[r, p:p + n] += hr
    return a, b


def binary_steepest(h0, n, deadline, max_sweeps=200):
    h = to_binary(h0, n).astype(float)
    c = corr_d(h)
    cur = float(np.max(c))
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_count = cur
    sweeps = 0
    st0 = n - 1
    block = 12 if n <= 450 else 8

    while time.time() < deadline and sweeps < max_sweeps:
        sweeps += 1
        zeros = np.where(h == 0)[0]
        ones = np.where(h == 1)[0]
        if zeros.size == 0 or ones.size == 0:
            break
        a, b = build_ab(zeros, ones, h)
        local_best = cur
        best_i = -1
        best_j = -1

        for st in range(0, len(zeros), block):
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            en = min(st + block, len(zeros))
            ab = a[st:en]
            m = c[None, None, :] + ab[:, :, None] + b[None, :, :]
            m[:, :, st0] -= 2.0
            scores = m.max(axis=2)
            flat = scores.ravel()
            k = min(40, flat.size)
            if k < flat.size:
                part = np.argpartition(flat, k - 1)[:k]
                part = part[np.argsort(flat[part])]
            else:
                part = np.arange(k)

            for ci in part:
                ri = int(ci) // ones.size
                bi = int(ci) % ones.size
                i = int(zeros[st + ri])
                j = int(ones[bi])
                base = c + a[st + ri] + b[bi]
                base[st0] -= 2.0
                base[st0 + j - i] += 1.0
                base[st0 + i - j] += 1.0
                nm = float(base.max())
                if nm < local_best - 1e-12:
                    local_best = nm
                    best_i, best_j = i, j

        if best_i < 0:
            break
        h[best_i] = 1.0
        h[best_j] = 0.0
        c = corr_d(h)
        cur = float(np.max(c))
        if cur < best_count:
            best_count = cur
            best_h = h.copy()

    return best_h, best_count


def interpolate_h(h, n, n2):
    x1 = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    x2 = (np.arange(n2) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n2)
    h2 = np.interp(x2, x1, h)
    return fix_feasible(h2, n2)


def soft_project(z, beta, target):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=float)
    beta = max(float(beta), 1e-3)
    lo = float(np.min(z)) - 12.0 * beta
    hi = float(np.max(z)) + 12.0 * beta
    if hi <= lo:
        hi = lo + 1.0
    for _ in range(40):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        y = np.clip((z - mid) / beta, -50.0, 50.0)
        s = float(np.sum(expit(y)))
        if s > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
    y = np.clip((z - mid) / beta, -50.0, 50.0)
    return expit(y)


def soft_objective(x):
    h = soft_project(x, _BETA, _TARGET)
    c = corr_d(h)
    val, _ = logsum_count(c, _ALPHA)
    return float(val)


def soft_jacobian(x):
    h = soft_project(x, _BETA, _TARGET)
    c = corr_d(h)
    _, w = logsum_count(c, _ALPHA)
    g = make_grad(h, w, _N)
    p = h * (1.0 - h)
    sp = float(p.sum())
    if sp < 1e-14:
        return np.zeros(_N)
    mean = float((g * p).sum() / sp)
    return (p / _BETA) * (g - mean)


def soft_lbfgs(h, n, beta, alpha, maxfun, seed=None, noise=0.0):
    global _ALPHA, _BETA, _N, _TARGET
    n = int(n)
    _N = n
    _TARGET = 0.5 * n
    _BETA = max(float(beta), 1e-3)
    _ALPHA = float(alpha)

    hc = np.clip(h, 1e-5, 1.0 - 1e-5)
    z = _BETA * np.log(hc / (1.0 - hc))
    if noise:
        rng = np.random.RandomState(seed if seed is not None else 1)
        z += float(noise) * _BETA * rng.randn(n)
    z -= float(np.mean(z))
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        z = np.zeros(n)

    bounds = [(-20.0, 20.0)] * n
    opts = {
        'maxiter': int(max(50, max(50, maxfun) // 3)),
        'maxfun': int(max(50, maxfun)),
        'ftol': 1e-18,
        'gtol': 1e-10,
        'maxcor': 20,
        'maxls': 5,
        'disp': False,
    }
    try:
        res = minimize(soft_objective, z, jac=soft_jacobian, method='L-BFGS-B',
                       bounds=bounds, options=opts)
        h2 = soft_project(res.x, _BETA, _TARGET)
    except Exception:
        h2 = h
    return fix_feasible(h2, n)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + max(30.0, float(budget_s) - 10.0)

    try:
        n = int(len(initial_h_values))
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
    except Exception:
        n = 400
        h0 = make_initial(n, seed)

    h0 = fix_feasible(h0, n)
    best_h = h0.copy()
    best_cnt = float(np.max(corr_d(h0)))
    best_c = best_cnt * (2.0 / n)

    for cand in (1.0 - h0, h0[::-1], 1.0 - h0[::-1]):
        cand = fix_feasible(cand, n)
        cnt = float(np.max(corr_d(cand)))
        if np.isfinite(cnt) and cnt < best_cnt:
            best_cnt = cnt
            best_c = cnt * (2.0 / n)
            best_h = cand.copy()

    total = max(1.0, deadline - t0)

    p_bin = t0 + min(150.0, max(30.0, 0.18 * total))
    if p_bin > deadline:
        p_bin = deadline
    if time.time() < p_bin - 5.0:
        hb, bcnt = binary_steepest(best_h, n, p_bin - 5.0, max_sweeps=200)
        if np.isfinite(bcnt) and bcnt < best_cnt:
            best_cnt = bcnt
            best_c = bcnt * (2.0 / n)
            best_h = hb.copy()
        if time.time() < p_bin - 25.0:
            h_alt = make_initial(n, seed + 101)
            hb2, bcnt2 = binary_steepest(h_alt, n, p_bin - 25.0, max_sweeps=80)
            if np.isfinite(bcnt2) and bcnt2 < best_cnt:
                best_cnt = bcnt2
                best_c = bcnt2 * (2.0 / n)
                best_h = hb2.copy()

    cont_dead = deadline - max(90.0, 0.18 * total)
    if total < 300.0:
        cont_dead = deadline - 10.0

    if time.time() < cont_dead - 30.0:
        hwork = best_h.copy()
        plan = [
            (1.0, 10.0, 0.0),
            (0.5, 100.0, 0.0),
            (0.25, 1000.0, 0.0),
            (0.12, 10000.0, 0.0),
            (0.06, 100000.0, 0.0),
            (0.03, 1000000.0, 0.0),
            (0.06, 100000.0, 0.05),
            (0.03, 1000000.0, 0.05),
        ]
        for idx, (beta, alpha, noise) in enumerate(plan):
            if time.time() > cont_dead - 30.0:
                break
            mf = 450 if n <= 450 else 260
            if cont_dead - time.time() < 150.0:
                mf = 160
            hnew = soft_lbfgs(hwork, n, beta, alpha, mf, seed + idx, noise)
            hnew = fix_feasible(hnew, n)
            cnt = float(np.max(corr_d(hnew)))
            if np.isfinite(cnt) and cnt < best_cnt:
                best_cnt = cnt
                best_c = cnt * (2.0 / n)
                best_h = hnew.copy()
                hwork = best_h.copy()
            elif np.isfinite(cnt) and cnt < best_cnt + 0.05:
                hwork = hnew.copy()

        if time.time() < cont_dead - 30.0:
            hb = to_binary(best_h, n)
            hnew = soft_lbfgs(hb, n, 0.06, 100000.0, 260, seed + 40, 0.03)
            hnew = fix_feasible(hnew, n)
            cnt = float(np.max(corr_d(hnew)))
            if np.isfinite(cnt) and cnt < best_cnt:
                best_cnt = cnt
                best_c = cnt * (2.0 / n)
                best_h = hnew.copy()

    higher = []
    for delta in (100, 200):
        nt = n + delta
        if nt <= 800 and nt not in higher:
            higher.append(nt)

    for n_try in higher:
        if time.time() > deadline - 80.0:
            break
        hwork = interpolate_h(best_h, n, n_try)
        for beta, alpha in ((0.15, 10000.0), (0.06, 100000.0)):
            if time.time() > deadline - 45.0:
                break
            hnew = soft_lbfgs(hwork, n_try, beta, alpha, 240, seed + n_try, 0.0)
            hnew = fix_feasible(hnew, n_try)
            cnt = float(np.max(corr_d(hnew)))
            if not np.isfinite(cnt):
                continue
            cval = cnt * (2.0 / n_try)
            if cval < best_c:
                best_c = cval
                best_h = hnew.copy()
                best_cnt = cnt
                n = n_try
                hwork = best_h.copy()
            elif cval < best_c + 0.05 * (2.0 / n_try):
                hwork = hnew.copy()

    final_plan = [
        (0.03, 1.0e6, 400, 0.0),
        (0.02, 1.0e7, 250, 0.0),
        (0.06, 1.0e5, 250, 0.02),
    ]
    for idx, (beta, alpha, mf, noise) in enumerate(final_plan):
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        if rem < 30.0:
            break
        mf_use = int(min(mf, 160 if rem < 100.0 else mf))
        hnew = soft_lbfgs(best_h, n, beta, alpha, mf_use, seed + 900 + idx, noise)
        hnew = fix_feasible(hnew, n)
        cnt = float(np.max(corr_d(hnew)))
        if np.isfinite(cnt):
            cval = cnt * (2.0 / n)
            if cval < best_c:
                best_c = cval
                best_cnt = cnt
                best_h = hnew.copy()

    hs = snap_feasible(best_h, n)
    cnts = float(np.max(corr_d(hs)))
    if np.isfinite(cnts):
        csv = cnts * (2.0 / n)
        if csv < best_c:
            best_c = csv
            best_cnt = cnts
            best_h = hs.copy()

    best_h = fix_feasible(best_h, n)
    final_cnt = float(np.max(corr_d(best_h)))
    best_c = final_cnt * (2.0 / n)
    return best_h, float(best_c), int(n)
```
